([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-input-preparation))
- **bam-alignment-merging**: Use when a hybrid RNA-seq dataset (e.g. PacBio PRJEB36569 plus Illumina PRJEB36584 aligned to galGal6) has produced separate per-technology BAM files and a genome-guided assembly input is needed for StringTie. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-alignment-merging))
- **bam-alignment-merging-across-aligners**: Use when RNA-seq reads have been aligned independently by Hisat2 and STAR and a downstream integrative assembler (e.g., Tiglon) needs a combined BAM or a bamFile_list of the per-aligner BAMs as input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-merging-across-aligners))
- **bam-alignment-parsing-with-pysam**: Use when you have a gRNA-aligned BAM file (e.g. gRNA.sorted.mapped.removedSecondaryAlignment.onlyMappedToGrnaChrom.bam) plus a filtered cell barcode list and need per-UMI consensus gRNA sequences for mutation calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-parsing-with-pysam))
- **bam-alignment-preprocessing**: Use when starting from cellranger/STAR-aligned scRNA-seq BAMs and you need a combined, per-strand alignment to feed annotation-free TAR discovery or to compute the coverage cutoff n. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-preprocessing))
- **bam-alignment-processing**: Use when sorted, length-filtered RiboSeq BAM files (e.g. BAM.25-35 folder) and a GFF annotation are available and a gene-level count matrix is needed for downstream DESeq2 differential expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-processing--ecad5b4126ff1679))
- **bam-alignment-processing**: Use when you have long-read cDNA/direct-RNA reads (FASTQ) plus a reference FASTA and need a splice-aware BAM alignment as input for isoform detection, quantification and tool benchmarking against a known isoform annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-processing--3ab785bf87416dff))
- **bam-alignment-processing**: Use when a sorted BAM of small-RNA reads aligned to a tRNA-masked artificial genome and a matching reference FASTA are available and the goal is to predict modified nucleoside sites along tncRNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-processing--31a528473fcde9ee))
- **bam-alignment-qc-reporting**: Use when you have sorted, indexed alignment BAM files (e.g. ONT cDNA or Illumina TruSeq alignments against a plasmid/mRNA reference FASTA) and need automated QC reports covering coverage homogeneity, per-nucleotide error rates, and consensus accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-alignment-qc-reporting))
- **bam-alignment-read-parsing**: Use when input is haplotype-resolved ONT/PacBio BAM alignments and the goal is de novo detection of candidate repetitive regions without known motifs or prior TR annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-alignment-read-parsing))
- **bam-alignment-sorting-validation**: Use when a BAM file is about to be fed into CRIS (or an analogous RNA-seq Ig reconstruction pipeline) and you must verify or produce hg38/GRCh38 alignment, coordinate sorting, and an index before running. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-alignment-sorting-validation))
- **bam-api**: Query BAM API interface definitions, endpoint lists, version information, or create new versions by PSM, method name, path, or endpoint ID. Use this whenever the user wants to look up API definitions, check interface documentation, find endpoint details, list a service's APIs, browse API versions, or create a new BAM version. Also trigger when the user needs to understand what APIs a service exposes, find a specific method signature, or publish a new version — this is for reading API metadata and version management, not for sending actual requests (use bam-query for that). ([yuezhen-huang/my-bytedance-skillhub](https://github.com/yuezhen-huang/my-bytedance-skillhub/tree/HEAD/bam-api) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **bam-cigar-softclip-parsing**: Use when a BAM of aligned sequencing reads (e.g. 10x Chromium 3'-enriched data) must be mined for soft-clipped reads bearing polyA-tails to derive per-position truncation-point tallies for 3' UTR end determination. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-cigar-softclip-parsing))
- **bam-coordinate-strand-filtering**: Use when you have a sorted, indexed BAM of reads aligned to a plasmid or mRNA reference and need to partition reads into on-target mRNA product versus off-target classes (upstream, downstream, antisense, cryptic TSS, E. coli contaminants) with fractional abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-coordinate-strand-filtering))
- **bam-downsampling-normalization**: Use when comparing two spike-in ChIP-seq conditions (e.g. H3K79me2 25:75 vs 75:25 mixed Jurkat cells) with unequal fly spike-in read totals, and you need to equalize spike-in depth by downsampling the human BAM, or you need a baseline uniform-scaling normalization to benchmark against spikChIP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-downsampling-normalization))
- **bam-file-construction-and-merging**: Use when benchmarking allele-specific CNA callers requires simulated single-cell scWGS BAMs with known ground-truth events (200 kb–5 Mb), preserved haplotype structure, and realistic amplification artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-construction-and-merging))
- **bam-file-coordinate-sorting-verification**: Use when before invoking pp.make_fragment_file on a BAM file from alignment or external sources, especially when the BAM's sort order is unknown or when integrating BAM files from multiple sequencing platforms (10X, standard genomics pipelines, or custom aligners) into a unified SnapATAC2 analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/bam-file-coordinate-sorting-verification))
- **bam-file-generation**: Use when linker-filtered ChIA-PET/HiChIP FASTQ tags must be mapped to a reference genome and reduced to unique, nonredundant PET alignments (MAPQ ≥ 30) in a BAM for downstream peak and loop calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-generation))
- **bam-file-generation-from-mapped-reads**: Use when raw fastq files (e.g. H3K4me3 ChIPseq or RNAseq from mouse embryonic stem cells) must be aligned as an automated pipeline step and .bam outputs are needed for downstream QC, filtering, or peak calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-generation-from-mapped-reads))
- **bam-file-merging**: Use when a basecalling stage has produced multiple per-input BAM files (one per POD5) that must be combined into one unmapped BAM before mapping or modification extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-file-merging))
- **bam-file-parsing-and-read-pair-joining**: Use when a metagenomic read-mapping .bam file must be filtered before profile coverage/SNV analysis and paired-end read structure (orientation, insert size, mapQ) should drive filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/bam-file-parsing-and-read-pair-joining))
- **bam-file-processing**: Use when adapter-trimmed plant sRNA-seq reads must be aligned to a masked/artificial tRNA genome and the resulting sorted BAM fed to HAMR or tncRNA classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-file-processing))
- **bam-file-processing**: Use when input is a single 10x Genomics barcoded BAM plus a barcodes.tsv.gz from outs/filtered_feature_bc_matrix/ and per-cell BAM files are needed for gene body coverage or per-cell QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-processing))
- **bam-file-processing-and-indexing**: Use when long-read (ONT/PacBio) SAM alignments from minimap2 must be converted, coordinate-sorted and indexed into BAM before breakpoint browsing in AgileStructure or IGV, e.g. for tandem-duplication annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-file-processing-and-indexing))
- **bam-file-read-downsampling**: Use when you need to determine the minimum sequencing depth at which a ChIP-seq mark's genome-wide profile becomes visually distinguishable, by comparing BedGraph profiles generated from BAM files sub-sampled to controlled read counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-read-downsampling))
- **bam-file-splitting-by-cell-barcode**: Use when the input is a single 10x Genomics barcoded BAM (e.g. possorted_genome_bam.bam) plus a barcodes.tsv.gz from outs/filtered_feature_bc_matrix/, and per-cell BAM files are needed before gene body coverage analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-splitting-by-cell-barcode))
- **bam-file-strand-separation**: Use when input is a strand-specific aligned BAM (e.g. Tb927_01_v5.1.slice.bam) and the next step is per-strand coverage/peak calling toward 3' UTR annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-file-strand-separation))
- **BAM-filtration**: Performs data cleaning and removal operations. This skill takes a raw BAM and creates a new, "clean" BAM file by actively removing artifacts: mitochondrial reads, blacklisted regions, PCR duplicates, and unmapped reads. Use this skill to "clean," "filter," or "remove bad reads" from a dataset. This is a prerequisite step before peak calling. Do NOT use this skill if you only want to view statistics without modifying the file. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/development/bam-filtration-majiayu000) · ★67 · 2 copies)
- **bam-granges-wiggle-file-size-profiling**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking an NGS pipeline's runtime and output file-size footprint on truncated ChIPseq/RNAseq test data, or validating that produced bam/GRanges/wig files match expected size regimes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-granges-wiggle-file-size-profiling))
- **bam-indexing-and-sorting**: Use when mapped reads (BAM) must be filtered by fragment-size thresholds, deduplicated, sorted, and indexed before ChIPseq/RNAseq peak calling in an NEAT-style pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-indexing-and-sorting))
- **bam-junction-read-support-quantification**: Use when you have short-read RNA-seq alignments (BAM) from two or more splice-aware aligners (Hisat2, STAR) and need to quantify junction read support and assign aligner-capture labels to splice graph edges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-junction-read-support-quantification))
- **bam-level-allele-specific-read-injection**: Use when a CNA-caller benchmark needs controlled ground truth: inject a known allele-specific chr3 copy loss into real 10x 5' scRNA-seq data and measure AUROC-based detection recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-level-allele-specific-read-injection))
- **bam-mapping-quality-reporting**: Use when trimmed nanopore FASTQ reads should be aligned to a supplied reference sequence (params.ref) to generate alignment statistics and coverage reports prior to consensus genome reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-mapping-quality-reporting))
- **bam-merge-with-picard**: Use when you have primary short-read alignments from NextGenMap plus re-aligned soft-clipped reads from YAHA and must combine them into one BAM before SV calling or coverage normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-merge-with-picard))
- **bam-parsing-coverage-statistics**: Use when validating metagenome contig assemblies (e.g., MeGAMerge MergedContigs.fasta or individual CLC bio/Velvet/SOAPdenovo assemblies) by quantifying what fraction of contig bases are covered by mapped reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-parsing-coverage-statistics))
- **bam-quality-filtered-coverage-computation**: Use when you have one or more aligned BAM files (e.g. nanopore reads aligned by minimap2, NGMLR, or lra) and need MAPQ-thresholded genome coverage fractions, median depth, or per-base depth to characterize a dataset or support coverage-based SV refinement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-quality-filtered-coverage-computation))
- **bam-query**: Primary tool for RPC and HTTP interface testing via BAM. It supports auto-defaulting for several parameters, so YOU MUST show the red-line parameters (PSM, API, IDL Version, VRegion, Env, Cluster, Request Body, and for HTTP also Method, Query Params, Headers, Cookies if provided) to the user for confirmation before execution. ([yuezhen-huang/my-bytedance-skillhub](https://github.com/yuezhen-huang/my-bytedance-skillhub/tree/HEAD/bam-query) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **bam-read-alignment-processing**: Use when a GFF3/GTF canonical annotation must be extended with 3' UTRs using aligned reads (BAM) enriched at transcript 3' ends, e.g. 10x Chromium scRNA-seq BAMs with ~10^8 reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-alignment-processing))
- **bam-read-assignment-by-haplotype**: Use when you have a mapped nanopore BAM plus a phased VCF for the same sample and need to separate reads into haplotype-specific sets, e.g. for allele-specific methylation analysis on HG002 R10.4 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-read-assignment-by-haplotype))
- **bam-read-duplicate-removal**: Use when raw ChIP-seq BAM alignments (e.g. SHR/BN rat H3K27me3 or CD-1 mouse H3K9me3 data) are about to be aggregated into fixed-width bins for downstream read-count-based modeling, and duplicates have not yet been removed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-duplicate-removal))
- **bam-read-fetching-by-region**: Use when haplotype-resolved long-read BAM files (e.g., minimap2-aligned ONT or PacBio alignments) and a BED file of candidate repeat regions are available and reads fully spanning each region must be extracted for POA consensus building. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-read-fetching-by-region))
- **bam-read-filtering-and-splitting**: Use when raw ChIP-seq FASTQ reads have been mapped to a combined sample+spike-in genome and unaligned reads must be removed before per-bin counting or human/fly separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-filtering-and-splitting))
- **bam-read-filtering-by-gene-assignment**: Use when starting from a CellRanger or STAR scRNA-Seq BAM and you need unassigned (non-gene) reads to quantify locus-specific transposable element expression without gene-contamination artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-filtering-by-gene-assignment))
- **bam-read-pileup-filtering**: Use when preparing long-read BAM alignments for Longshot candidate SNV discovery and you must enforce Longshot's read-level (min_mapq) and position-level (min_cov, max_cov) filters before allelotyping or genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-read-pileup-filtering))
- **bam-read-processing-for-visualization**: Use when raw ChIP-seq or RNA-seq data must be mapped, cleaned, and packaged with chromosome/refGene annotation files so SeqCode commands like buildChIPprofile, produceTSSplots, or produceTSSmaps can generate plots and heatmaps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-processing-for-visualization))
- **bam-read-subsampling-and-quality-filtering**: Use when ChIP-seq/DNase-seq replicate BAMs must be normalized to equal read depth before QC metrics (FRiP, PBC, replicate correlation) so comparisons across replicates are not biased by library size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-subsampling-and-quality-filtering))
- **bam-read-total-quantification**: Use when running spike-in ChIP-seq normalization (e.g. ChIP-Rx in spikChIP) where each human bin count must be divided by total fly (dm3) spike-in reads, or when BAM totals must be reported or used for downsampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/bam-read-total-quantification))
- **bam-sorting-and-indexing**: Use when basecalled DRS reads have been aligned to a reference transcriptome (e.g. with minimap2) and downstream steps need a sorted, indexed BAM — such as samtools view filtering, jvarkit sam2tsv conversion, or feature extraction in RedNano. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-sorting-and-indexing--85eb22ccc1cd8079))
- **bam-sorting-and-indexing**: Use when raw SAM alignments from Nanopore/cDNA/dRNA reads must be converted to sorted, indexed BAM before read-fate QC, per-nucleotide error profiling, or genome-viewer inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-sorting-and-indexing--d71cdb85c1e3a8b4))
- **bam-sorting-and-indexing**: Reference for converting merged SAM alignments from Ribo-seq double-mapping (HISAT2 splice-aware followed by Bowtie2 mismatch-tolerant) into sorted, indexed BAM/BAI files ready for read counting and periodicity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-sorting-and-indexing))
- **bam-splice-coordinate-extraction**: Use when a BAM/GFF3/BED6 long-read RNA alignment must be converted into a consensus set of splice-site/exon-boundary coordinates per gene, prior to isoform assembly and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-splice-coordinate-extraction))
- **bam-split-read-parsing**: Use when an indexed BAM of long reads (e.g. ONT reads aligned with minimap2 -ax map-ont --secondary=no) must be scanned for split-read junctions to locate large-scale genomic rearrangement breakpoints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-split-read-parsing))
- **bam-statistics**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Sequence_Analysis/alignment-files/bam-statistics) · ★7)
- **bam-statistics**: Generate alignment statistics using samtools flagstat, stats, depth, coverage, and mosdepth. Use when assessing alignment quality, calculating coverage, or generating QC reports. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/alignment-files/bam-statistics) · ★1)
- **bam-tag-parsing-cb-ub**: Use when somatic SNVs from eWGS must be detected per-cell in 10x 5' scRNA-seq BAMs by pileup over reads carrying valid CB and UB_tag values, producing per-cell mutant counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-tag-parsing-cb-ub))
- **bam-tag-parsing-chromium-barcode**: Use when analyzing 10x Chromium scRNA-seq alignments (BAMs with CB/UB tags) or _counts_UB.tsv tables and you need per-cell, per-UMI molecule counts collapsed by genomic position — e.g., to profile coverage decay distal to the capture site. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/bam-tag-parsing-chromium-barcode))
- **bam-to-bigwig-conversion**: Use when after running TOBIAS ATACorrect to generate bias-corrected signal tracks from aligned ATAC-seq reads. Use this skill when you have corrected cutsite signal (as . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/bam-to-bigwig-conversion))
- **bam-to-fasta-conversion**: Use when a FLNC read set produced by PacBio isoseq3 refine exists only as a BAM file and downstream steps (e.g. TAMA gstama_polyacleanup) require FASTA input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/bam-to-fasta-conversion))
- **bam-to-fragment-file-conversion**: Use when when you have coordinate-sorted BAM files from single-cell ATAC-seq experiments (e.g., 10X Genomics scATAC-seq) and need to generate a compressed fragment file for efficient downstream analysis. Use this step before matrix generation, clustering, or peak calling in SnapATAC2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/bam-to-fragment-file-conversion))
- **bam-vcf-haplotag-post-processing**: Use when meth_phaser_parallel has finished writing block-relationship outputs into a work directory and the re-phased VCF and haplotag-updated BAM are needed to complete the phasing workflow.
